    function updateSongPill() {
      const song = RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx];
      const el = document.getElementById('songPill');
      if (!el || !song) return;
      el.textContent = feastComplete && currentSongIdx > 0
        ? `♫ encore · ${song.bpm} bpm`
        : `♫ ${song.title} · ${song.bpm} bpm`;
    }

    const QUOTE_SPEAKERS = ['sarah', 'ninjawhee', 'orph', 'simon', 'honey', 'the store'];

    let quoteCyclerPaused = false;

    function stopQuoteCycler() {
      clearInterval(quoteCyclerTimer);
      clearInterval(quoteTypeTimer);
      clearTimeout(quotePauseTimer);
      quoteCyclerTimer = null;
      quoteTypeTimer = null;
      quotePauseTimer = null;
      quoteCyclerPaused = false;
      document.getElementById('quotePanel')?.classList.remove('intense');
    }

    function pauseQuoteCycler(ms = 900) {
      if (quoteCyclerPaused) return;
      quoteCyclerPaused = true;
      clearInterval(quoteTypeTimer);
      document.getElementById('quotePanel')?.classList.add('intense');
      clearTimeout(quotePauseTimer);
      quotePauseTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        quotePauseTimer = null;
        quoteCyclerPaused = false;
        document.getElementById('quotePanel')?.classList.remove('intense');
        if (quoteCyclerTimer && running) {
          const el = document.getElementById('quoteTyped');
          const full = QUOTES[quoteCycleIdx];
          if (el && el.textContent.length < full.length) typeQuoteLine(full);
        }
      }, ms);
    }

    function typeQuoteLine(text) {
      const el = document.getElementById('quoteTyped');
      if (!el) return;
      quoteCharIdx = 0;
      el.textContent = '';
      clearInterval(quoteTypeTimer);
      const speaker = QUOTE_SPEAKERS[quoteCycleIdx % QUOTE_SPEAKERS.length];
      const speakerEl = document.getElementById('quoteSpeaker');
      if (speakerEl) speakerEl.textContent = `— ${speaker}`;
      quoteTypeTimer = setInterval(() => {
        if (quoteCyclerPaused) return;
        if (quoteCharIdx >= text.length) {
          clearInterval(quoteTypeTimer);
          quoteTypeTimer = null;
          return;
        }
        el.textContent += text[quoteCharIdx++];
      }, 28);
    }

    function startQuoteCycler() {
      stopQuoteCycler();
      quoteCycleIdx = 0;
      typeQuoteLine(QUOTES[0]);
      quoteCyclerTimer = setInterval(() => {
        quoteCycleIdx = (quoteCycleIdx + 1) % QUOTES.length;
        typeQuoteLine(QUOTES[quoteCycleIdx]);
      }, 14000);
    }

    function syncLayout() {
      const margin = Math.max(48, W * 0.08);
      const playW = W - margin * 2;
      laneX0 = margin;
      laneW = playW / 4;
      laneTopY = H * LANE_TOP;
      hitY = H * LANE_BOTTOM;
      cx = W / 2;

      HeavyRuntime.syncLaneLayout(root, {
        W, margin, laneW, laneX0, hitY,
        laneCenters: [0, 1, 2, 3].map(laneCenter),
      });
    }

    function resize() {
      W = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      H = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      syncLayout();
      stars = Array.from({ length: 70 }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * W, y: Math.random() * H,
        r: Math.random() > 0.7 ? 3 : 2, tw: Math.random() * 6.28, sp: 0.008 + Math.random() * 0.01
      }));
    }

    function laneCenter(lane) { return laneX0 + lane * laneW + laneW / 2; }

    function noteFallProgress(n, now) {
      const elapsed = now - (n.time - approachTime);
      return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, elapsed / approachTime));
    }

    function noteY(n, now) {
      return laneTopY + (hitY - laneTopY) * noteFallProgress(n, now);
    }

    function scheduleNextPizzaSpawn(fromBeat) {
      const song = RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx];
      const minB = song?.pizzaMinBeats ?? PIZZA_MIN_BEATS;
      const maxB = song?.pizzaMaxBeats ?? PIZZA_MAX_BEATS;
      const gap = minB + Math.floor(Math.random() * (maxB - minB + 1));
      nextPizzaSpawnBeat = fromBeat + gap;
    }

    function maybeSpawnBonusPizza(now) {
      const beat = now / beatMs;
      if (beat < 18) return;
      if (bonusPizzas.some((p) => !p.hit && !p.missed)) return;
      if (beat < nextPizzaSpawnBeat) return;
      const lane = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);
      bonusPizzas.push({
        lane, time: nextPizzaSpawnBeat * beatMs, hit: false, missed: false, isPizza: true,
      });
      scheduleNextPizzaSpawn(nextPizzaSpawnBeat);
      pizzaHintUntil = performance.now() + 2200;
      if (!pizzaTutorialShown) {
        pizzaTutorialShown = true;
        showPhaseBrief('bonus pizza — press matching key at gold line!', 3200);
      } else {
        showPhaseBrief('bonus pizza slice!', 1400);
      }
    }

    function playPizzaBonusSfx(lane) {
      if (!audioCtx) return;
      duckRhythmSfx(0.14);
      const t = audioCtx.currentTime;
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const g = audioCtx.createGain();
      const f = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(520 + lane * 95, t);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880 + lane * 40, t + 0.12);
      f.type = 'lowpass';
      f.frequency.value = 2800;
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.22, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.38);
      osc.connect(f).connect(g).connect(sfxGain || masterGain);
      osc.start(t);
      osc.stop(t + 0.4);
      const crunch = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const cg = audioCtx.createGain();
      crunch.type = 'square';
      crunch.frequency.value = 140 + lane * 18;
      cg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, t + 0.05);
      cg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.22);
      crunch.connect(cg).connect(sfxGain || masterGain);
      crunch.start(t + 0.05);
      crunch.stop(t + 0.24);
    }

    function tryHitBonusPizza(lane, now) {
      let best = null;
      let bestDiff = Infinity;
      for (const p of bonusPizzas) {
        if (p.lane !== lane || p.hit || p.missed) continue;
        if (now < p.time - HIT_LEAD_MS) continue;
        const diff = Math.abs(now - p.time);
        if (diff < winGoodMs * 1.08 && diff < bestDiff) { best = p; bestDiff = diff; }
      }
      if (!best) return false;
      best.hit = true;
      bonusPizzaEaten++;
      const bonus = 380 + combo * 12;
      score += bonus;
      combo = Math.min(99, combo + 2);
      improvMeter = Math.min(100, improvMeter + 8);
      document.getElementById('improvFill').style.width = improvMeter + '%';
      document.getElementById('improvLvl').textContent = Math.round(improvMeter);
      playPizzaBonusSfx(lane);
      showJudgment('PIZZA!', 'great');
      flashKey(lane, 'great');
      burst(laneCenter(lane), hitY, '#e8a04c', 20);
      floatNotes.push({ x: laneCenter(lane), y: hitY - 24, life: 1.5, glyph: '∴', color: '#e8a04c' });
      screenShake = Math.min(4.5, screenShake + 2.2);
      updateUI();
      return true;
    }

    function checkBonusPizzaMisses(now) {
      for (const p of bonusPizzas) {
        if (p.hit || p.missed) continue;
        if (now - p.time > winGoodMs + 120) {
          p.missed = true;
        }
      }
      bonusPizzas = bonusPizzas.filter((p) => !p.hit && (!p.missed || now - p.time < 800));
      if (bonusPizzas.length > 4) bonusPizzas.splice(0, bonusPizzas.length - 4);
    }

    function drawBonusPizza(p, now) {
      const y = noteY(p, now);
      const x = laneCenter(p.lane);
      const diff = Math.abs(now - p.time);
      const inWindow = now >= p.time - HIT_LEAD_MS && diff <= winGoodMs;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.92 + Math.sin(time * 0.25 + p.lane) * 0.08;
      const r = 22;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e8a04c';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x, y - r * 0.15);
      ctx.arc(x, y, r, -Math.PI * 0.78, Math.PI * 0.55);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#c45c7a';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x - r * 0.2, y - r * 0.1, r * 0.22, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(x + r * 0.15, y + r * 0.05, r * 0.18, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.font = '5px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1028';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText('PIZZA', x, y + 2);
      if (inWindow) {
        PixelGfx.fillPixelDisk(ctx, x, hitY, 7 + (Math.sin(time * 0.3) > 0 ? 2 : 0), 4, '#e8a04ccc');
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#f8f4ff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.strokeRect(x - r - 4, y - r - 4, r * 2 + 8, r * 2 + 8);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    function markFeastComplete() {
      if (feastComplete) return;
      feastComplete = true;
      encoreStartedAt = performance.now();
      encoreAdvanceCount = 0;
      showPhaseBrief('feast complete — fast songs incoming · [Z] mirror when ready', 4800);
      document.getElementById('encorePill')?.classList.add('visible');
      pauseQuoteCycler(1200);
    }

    function cleanupRhythmSession() {
      rhythmHandoffGen++;
      phaseBriefTimers.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
      phaseBriefTimers = [];
      activeNotes = [];
      chart = [];
      particles.length = 0;
      ripples.length = 0;
      floatNotes.length = 0;
      hitFlashes.length = 0;
      stars.length = 0;
      collectedColors.length = 0;
      bridgeFlashUntil = 0;
      echoRichRun = false;
      screenShake = 0;
      document.body.classList.remove('combo-hot');
      bonusPizzas = [];
      bonusPizzaEaten = 0;
      feastComplete = false;
      pizzaTutorialShown = false;
      pizzaHintUntil = 0;
      encoreStartedAt = 0;
      encoreAdvanceCount = 0;
      currentSongIdx = 0;
      difficultyTier = 0;
      pressureMode = 'steady';
      recentMissStreak = 0;
      recentPerfectStreak = 0;
      lastPressureInjectBeat = 0;
      adaptiveNoteId = 5000;
      nextPizzaSpawnBeat = PIZZA_FIRST_BEAT;
      beatMs = 60000 / BALLAD_BPM;
      approachTime = APPROACH_BASE_MS;
      songBeats = RHYTHM_SONGS[0].totalBeats;
      document.getElementById('encorePill')?.classList.remove('visible');
      document.getElementById('songPill').textContent = '';
      document.getElementById('pizzaHintBar')?.classList.remove('visible');
      audioBus?.snapGains?.();
    }

    async function ensureAudioReady() {
      initAudio();
      await audioBus?.resume();
    }

    function initAudio() {
      if (audioCtx) return;
      audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      audioBus = window.AudioBus?.create(audioCtx);
      masterGain = audioBus.masterGain;
      rhythmGain = audioBus.rhythmGain;
      sfxGain = audioBus.sfxGain;
      rhythmLoop = window.RhythmLoop?.create(audioCtx, rhythmGain);
      VinylAudio?.init(audioCtx, audioBus.vinylGain);
      VinylAudio?.onStop?.(() => {
        if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
          JazzStoreOverworld.clearListening?.();
        }
      });
      audioBus.registerHandlers({
        stopVinyl: (fadeSec = 0.28) => {
          VinylEchoBridge?.cleanup?.();
          if (VinylAudio?.isPlaying?.() && VinylAudio?.fadeOut) VinylAudio.fadeOut(fadeSec);
          else VinylAudio?.hardStop?.() ?? VinylAudio?.stop?.();
        },
        stopRhythm: () => {
          rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
          cleanupRhythmSession();
        },
        stopEcho: () => VinylEchoBridge?.cleanup?.(),
        stopQuotes: () => stopQuoteCycler(),
      });
      window.__eatItemPickupBlip = () => {
        if (!audioCtx || audioCtx.state === 'suspended') return;
        HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, sfxGain || masterGain, 'choice');
      };
      window.__eatAmbientBlip = () => {
        if (!audioCtx || audioCtx.state === 'suspended') return;
        HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, sfxGain || masterGain, 'talk', 420);
      };
    }

    function duckRhythmSfx(holdSec = 0.13) {
      audioBus?.duckRhythm(holdSec);
    }

    const GROK_PORTRAIT_SRC = 'assets/ninjawhee-grok-pixel-clean.png';
    HeavyDialogueArt.loadGrokPortrait(GROK_PORTRAIT_SRC);

    function playImprov(lane) {
      duckRhythmSfx(0.11);
      HeavyRuntime.playJazzImprov(audioCtx, sfxGain, lane, PENTATONIC, improvStep, LANE_DEGREE);

      ripples.push({ lane, y: hitY, life: 1, maxR: laneW * 0.42 });
      if (ripples.length > 20) ripples.splice(0, ripples.length - 20);
      floatNotes.push({
        x: laneCenter(lane), y: hitY - 20,
        life: 1, glyph: ['♪', '♫', '𝅗𝅥', '∿'][lane], color: LANES[lane].color
      });
      improvMeter = Math.min(100, improvMeter + (grooveMode ? 6 : 4));
      document.getElementById('improvFill').style.width = improvMeter + '%';
      document.getElementById('improvLvl').textContent = Math.round(improvMeter);
      const cap = document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${lane}"]`);
      cap.classList.add('improv');
      setTimeout(() => cap.classList.remove('improv'), 120);
    }

    function playLaneHit(lane, judgment) {
      duckRhythmSfx(judgment === 'perfect' ? 0.16 : 0.12);
      if (judgment === 'perfect' || combo >= 4) pauseQuoteCycler(700);
      HeavyRuntime.playJudgmentSfx(audioCtx, sfxGain, lane, judgment, LANES[lane].pitch);
      const rippleScale = judgment === 'perfect' ? 0.72 : judgment === 'great' ? 0.58 : 0.48;
      ripples.push({
        lane, y: hitY, life: 1, maxR: laneW * rippleScale, hit: true,
        color: judgment === 'perfect' ? '#f8f4ff' : judgment === 'great' ? '#4a8f7a' : '#c9a84c',
      });
      if (ripples.length > 24) ripples.splice(0, ripples.length - 24);
    }

    function getMicroQuotePool() {
      return [...MICRO_QUOTES, ...(window.EasterEggs?.getBonusMicroQuotes?.() || [])];
    }

    function eggPhase() {
      if (dialogue.active || mirrorChoiceOpen) return 'dialogue';
      if (running) return 'rhythm';
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) return 'overworld';
      return 'idle';
    }

    function handleEggUnlock(result, { dialogueNode = null, blip = true } = {}) {
      if (!result) return;
      if (blip) playConfirmBlip();
      const toast = result.toast;
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.(toast);
        if (dialogueNode) {
          if (dialogue.active) closeDialogueUI();
          openDialogueUI('secrets', dialogueNode, 'overworld', 'ninjawhee');
        }
      } else {
        showPhaseBrief(toast, 2800);
        if (dialogueNode && !running && !mirrorChoiceOpen) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (running || mirrorChoiceOpen || dialogue.active) return;
            openDialogueUI('secrets', dialogueNode, 'cinematic', 'ninjawhee');
          }, 900);
        }
      }
    }

    function onOverworldSecret(spot) {
      const hit = EasterEggs?.onOverworldSpot?.(spot);
      if (hit) {
        StorePause?.onSecret?.(spot, EasterEggs.META[spot]?.quote);
      }
      const dialogueNode = hit ? spot : null;
      if (hit) handleEggUnlock(hit, { dialogueNode: dialogueNode === 'moon_window' ? 'moon_window' : dialogueNode === 'mirror_door' ? 'mirror_door' : dialogueNode === 'counter_knock' ? 'counter_knock' : null });
      else if (EasterEggs?.has?.(spot)) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.(EasterEggs.META[spot]?.quote || '∴');
      }
      return true;
    }

    function getEchoChartSections(sections) {
      const names = {
        moon: 'soliloquy w/ moon',
        shelter: 'shelter from the storm',
        mirror: 'mirror at the edge',
        eat: 'eat the sounds',
      };
      return sections.map((s, i) => {
        const label = i === 1 && echoSeed?.vinyls?.[0]
          ? `${names[echoSeed.vinyls[0]] || s.label} · echoed`
          : i === 2 && echoSeed?.vinyls?.[1]
            ? `${names[echoSeed.vinyls[1]] || s.label} · echoed`
            : s.label;
        return { ...s, label };
      });
    }

    function applySongTiming(song) {
      beatMs = 60000 / song.bpm;
      approachTime = Math.round(APPROACH_BASE_MS * BALLAD_BPM / song.bpm);
      songBeats = song.totalBeats;
      rhythmLoop?.setBpm?.(song.bpm);
      const loopStyle = song.bpm >= 120 ? 'burner' : song.bpm >= 100 ? 'swing' : 'ballad';
      rhythmLoop?.setStyle?.(loopStyle);
    }

    function computeDifficultyTier() {
      const total = hits + misses;
      if (total < 3) return difficultyTier;
      const accuracy = hits / total;
      const missRate = misses / total;
      if (missRate > 0.38 || recentMissStreak >= 3) {
        pressureMode = 'scramble';
        return Math.min(4, difficultyTier + 1);
      }
      if (accuracy > 0.82 && combo >= 6) {
        pressureMode = 'swing';
        return Math.min(4, difficultyTier + 1);
      }
      if (accuracy > 0.65 && combo >= 3) {
        pressureMode = 'swing';
        return Math.min(4, Math.max(difficultyTier, 1));
      }
      pressureMode = 'steady';
      return Math.max(0, difficultyTier - (accuracy > 0.7 ? 0 : 1));
    }

    function makeChartNote(n, i, songIdx, song, pool, echoOrbRich) {
      return {
        id: songIdx * 1000 + i, time: n.beat * beatMs, lane: n.lane,
        type: n.type || 'tap', duration: n.duration ? n.duration * beatMs : 0,
        color: LANES[n.lane].color, label: LANES[n.lane].label,
        tutorial: n.tutorial || null, sectionBeat: n.beat,
        sectionIdx: n.sectionIdx,
        countsTowardGoal: !!song.countsTowardGoal && !n.filler,
        filler: !!n.filler,
        echoRich: songIdx === 0 && echoRichRun && n.sectionIdx >= 2,
        echoOrbBoost: songIdx === 0 && echoOrbRich,
        micro: pool[i % pool.length],
        glyph: HIEROGLYPHS[i % HIEROGLYPHS.length],
        hit: false, missed: false, chewing: false,
      };
    }

    function injectAdaptiveNotes(rawNotes, tier, mode) {
      if (tier < 1) return rawNotes;
      const extras = [];
      const sorted = [...rawNotes].sort((a, b) => a.beat - b.beat);
      for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length - 1; i++) {
        const gap = sorted[i + 1].beat - sorted[i].beat;
        if (gap < 2) continue;
        if (mode === 'scramble' && gap >= 2 && i % 2 === 0) {
          const midBeat = sorted[i].beat + gap * 0.5;
          extras.push({
            beat: midBeat, lane: (sorted[i].lane + 1 + tier) % 4,
            type: 'tap', sectionIdx: sorted[i].sectionIdx, filler: true,
          });
        }
        if (mode === 'swing' && gap >= 3 && tier >= 2) {
          extras.push({
            beat: sorted[i].beat + 0.5, lane: (sorted[i].lane + 2) % 4,
            type: tier >= 3 && i % 4 === 0 ? 'hold' : 'tap',
            duration: tier >= 3 && i % 4 === 0 ? 1.5 : 0,
            sectionIdx: sorted[i].sectionIdx, filler: true,
          });
        }
        if (mode === 'swing' && gap >= 4 && tier >= 3 && i % 3 === 1) {
          extras.push({
            beat: sorted[i + 1].beat - 0.5, lane: (sorted[i + 1].lane + 1) % 4,
            type: 'tap', sectionIdx: sorted[i].sectionIdx, filler: true,
          });
        }
      }
      return [...sorted, ...extras].sort((a, b) => a.beat - b.beat);
    }

    function injectLivePressure(now) {
      const tier = difficultyTier;
      if (tier < 1) return;
      if (chart.filter((n) => n.filler).length >= 32) return;
      const beat = now / beatMs;
      if (beat - lastPressureInjectBeat < (pressureMode === 'scramble' ? 3 : 5)) return;
      if (pressureMode === 'scramble' && recentMissStreak >= 2) {
        lastPressureInjectBeat = beat;
        recentMissStreak = 0;
        const lane = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);
        chart.push(makeChartNote({
          beat: beat + 3.5, lane, type: 'tap', sectionIdx: -1, filler: true,
        }, adaptiveNoteId++, currentSongIdx, RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx], getMicroQuotePool(), false));
        chart.sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);
        return;
      }
      if (pressureMode === 'swing' && recentPerfectStreak >= 3 && combo >= 8) {
        lastPressureInjectBeat = beat;
        recentPerfectStreak = 0;
        const lane = combo % 4;
        chart.push(makeChartNote({
          beat: beat + 4, lane, type: 'tap', sectionIdx: -1, filler: true,
        }, adaptiveNoteId++, currentSongIdx, RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx], getMicroQuotePool(), false));
        chart.push(makeChartNote({
          beat: beat + 4.5, lane: (lane + 1) % 4, type: 'tap', sectionIdx: -1, filler: true,
        }, adaptiveNoteId++, currentSongIdx, RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx], getMicroQuotePool(), false));
        chart.sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);
      }
    }

    function buildChartForSong(songIdx) {
      const song = RHYTHM_SONGS[songIdx];
      if (!song) return;
      applySongTiming(song);
      const pool = getMicroQuotePool();
      const echoCount = echoSeed?.vinyls?.length || 0;
      const echoOrbRich = echoCount > 2;
      if (songIdx === 0) {
        echoRichRun = echoOrbRich;
        if (echoRichRun) echoScoreMult = Math.max(echoScoreMult, 1.28 + echoCount * 0.04);
      }
      difficultyTier = computeDifficultyTier();
      const sections = getEchoChartSections(song.sections);
      let raw = sections.flatMap((s, sectionIdx) =>
        s.notes.map((n) => ({ ...n, sectionIdx }))
      );
      raw = injectAdaptiveNotes(raw, difficultyTier, pressureMode);
      chart = raw.map((n, i) => makeChartNote(n, i, songIdx, song, pool, echoOrbRich));
      announcedPhases = new Set();
      lastPressureInjectBeat = 0;
      updateSongPill();
    }

    function buildChart() {
      currentSongIdx = 0;
      buildChartForSong(0);
    }

    function advanceToNextSong() {
      if (!feastComplete && currentSongIdx >= 0) {
        if (!finishWinStarted) endSong();
        return;
      }
      let nextIdx;
      if (currentSongIdx === 0) nextIdx = 1;
      else if (currentSongIdx === 1) nextIdx = 2;
      else nextIdx = 1;
      currentSongIdx = nextIdx;
      const song = RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx];
      buildChartForSong(currentSongIdx);
      activeNotes = [];
      nextPizzaSpawnBeat = currentSongIdx === 0 ? PIZZA_FIRST_BEAT : 14;
      songStart = performance.now();
      audioBus?.duckRhythm?.(0.45, 0.06);
      rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.();
      rhythmLoop?.setBpm?.(song.bpm);
      rhythmLoop?.setStyle?.(song.bpm >= 120 ? 'burner' : song.bpm >= 100 ? 'swing' : 'ballad');
      if (audioCtx) {
        HeavyRuntime.playSongHandoff?.(audioCtx, sfxGain, song.bpm);
        HeavyRuntime.playSarahEncoreStinger?.(audioCtx, sfxGain, currentSongIdx);
      }
      const handoffId = ++rhythmHandoffGen;
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (handoffId !== rhythmHandoffGen || !running) return;
        rhythmLoop?.start();
      }, 80);
      updateSongPill();
      bridgeFlashUntil = performance.now() + 520;
      screenShake = song.bpm >= 120 ? 4.2 : 2.8;
      const sarahHandoff = currentSongIdx === 1
        ? 'sarah hums: stay for the whole side....'
        : 'sarah hums: the burner remembers you....';
      showPhaseBrief(song.transition || song.title, 3400);
      setTimeout(() => showPhaseBrief(sarahHandoff, 2600), 700);
      setTimeout(() => showPhaseBrief(song.title, 2800), 3400);
      document.getElementById('encorePill')?.classList.add('visible');
      document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width = '0%';
    }

    function showPhaseBrief(text, ms = 2200) {
      const el = document.getElementById('phaseLabel');
      el.textContent = text;
      el.classList.add('visible');
      phaseBriefTimers.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
      phaseBriefTimers = [];
      clearTimeout(phaseHideTimer);
      phaseHideTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('visible'), ms);
      phaseBriefTimers.push(phaseHideTimer);
    }

    function checkPhases(now) {
      const song = RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx];
      if (!song) return;
      for (const section of getEchoChartSections(song.sections)) {
        const startBeat = section.notes[0]?.beat;
        if (startBeat === undefined || announcedPhases.has(startBeat)) continue;
        if (now >= (startBeat - 2) * beatMs) {
          announcedPhases.add(startBeat);
          if (!section.notes[0].tutorial) showPhaseBrief(section.label);
        }
      }
    }

    function cullActiveNotes() {
      while (activeNotes.length > MAX_ACTIVE_NOTES) {
        const stale = activeNotes.findIndex((n) => n.missed || (n.hit && !n.chewing));
        if (stale >= 0) activeNotes.splice(stale, 1);
        else activeNotes.shift();
      }
    }

    function spawnNotes(now) {
      while (chart.length && chart[0].time - approachTime <= now) {
        activeNotes.push({ ...chart.shift() });
        cullActiveNotes();
      }
    }

    function updateHint(now) {
      const pizzaBar = document.getElementById('pizzaHintBar');
      const bar = document.getElementById('hintBar');
      document.querySelectorAll('.key-cap').forEach((c) => c.classList.remove('target'));

      let nearestPizza = null;
      let bestPizzaDiff = Infinity;
      for (const p of bonusPizzas) {
        if (p.hit || p.missed) continue;
        const diff = Math.abs(now - p.time);
        if (diff <= winGoodMs * 1.1 && diff < bestPizzaDiff) {
          nearestPizza = p;
          bestPizzaDiff = diff;
        }
      }
      if (nearestPizza && pizzaBar) {
        pizzaBar.classList.add('visible');
        document.getElementById('pizzaHintKey').textContent = LANES[nearestPizza.lane].label;
        document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${nearestPizza.lane}"]`)?.classList.add('target');
      } else if (pizzaBar && pizzaHintUntil > performance.now()) {
        pizzaBar.classList.add('visible');
      } else if (pizzaBar) {
        pizzaBar.classList.remove('visible');
      }

      let nearest = null, bestFall = -1;
      for (const n of activeNotes) {
        if (n.hit || n.missed || n.chewing) continue;
        const fall = noteFallProgress(n, now);
        if (fall >= 0.72 && fall <= 1.08 && fall > bestFall) { nearest = n; bestFall = fall; }
      }
      if (nearest) {
        document.getElementById('hintKey').textContent = nearest.label;
        bar.classList.add('visible');
        bar.querySelector('.sub').textContent = nearest.tutorial || nearest.micro || 'bite when slice hits gold line';
        document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${nearest.lane}"]`)?.classList.add('target');
      } else bar.classList.remove('visible');
    }

    function showJudgment(text, cls) {
      const el = document.getElementById('judgment');
      el.textContent = text; el.className = `judgment show ${cls}`;
      clearTimeout(showJudgment._t);
      const ms = cls === 'perfect' ? 620 : cls === 'great' ? 520 : 480;
      showJudgment._t = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), ms);
    }

    function flashKey(lane, cls) {
      const cap = document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${lane}"]`);
      cap.classList.remove('perfect', 'great', 'good', 'miss');
      if (cls) cap.classList.add(cls);
      setTimeout(() => cap.classList.remove(cls), cls === 'perfect' ? 420 : cls === 'great' ? 340 : 300);
    }

    function syncTimingWindows() {
      const chill = GameProgress?.isChill();
      const runs = GameProgress?.getState?.()?.runs ?? 0;
      const staticGrace = GameProgress?.getAftermathTier?.() === 'static';
      const firstGrace = runs < 1;
      const grace = !chill && (firstGrace || staticGrace);
      const mult = (chill ? CHILL_MULT : 1) * (grace ? GRACE_MULT : 1);
      winPerfectMs = PERFECT_MS * mult;
      winGreatMs = GREAT_MS * mult;
      winGoodMs = echoGoodMs * mult;
      chillAutoMs = chill ? CHILL_AUTO_MS : (grace ? GRACE_AUTO_MS : 0);
      document.body.classList.toggle('chill-active', !!chill);
      document.body.classList.toggle('grace-active', !!grace);
      updateModePill();
    }

    function updateModePill() {
      const el = document.getElementById('modePill');
      if (!el) return;
      const parts = [];
      if (GameProgress?.isChill()) parts.push('chill');
      if (grooveMode) parts.push('groove');
      if (running && echoSeed?.vinyls?.length) parts.push(`${echoSeed.vinyls.length} echo`);
      el.textContent = parts.length ? parts.join(' · ') : '';
      el.className = 'mode-pill' + (grooveMode ? ' groove' : GameProgress?.isChill() ? ' chill' : '');
    }

    function formatAlbumBreakdown() {
      const b = GameProgress?.getAlbumBreakdown?.();
      if (!b) return '';
      return `album ${b.total}% — store ${b.explore} · rhythm ${b.rhythm} · wings ${b.mastery}`;
    }

    function formatAlbumBreakdownDetail() {
      const b = GameProgress?.getAlbumBreakdown?.();
      if (!b) {
        const pct = GameProgress?.getAlbumPct?.() ?? 0;
        return {
          headline: `album ${pct}%`,
          detail: 'store · rhythm · wings — keep listening',
        };
      }
      const wings = GameProgress.getState().wins;
      return {
        headline: `album ${b.total}% — you listened to ${b.exploreDetail ?? b.explore}% of the store`,
        detail: `store ${b.explore}% · rhythm ${b.rhythm}% · wings ${b.mastery}% · ${wings} wing${wings === 1 ? '' : 's'} won`,
      };
    }

    function mirrorChoiceConsequence(choice) {
      const b = GameProgress?.getAlbumBreakdown?.();
      const pct = b?.total ?? GameProgress?.getAlbumPct?.() ?? 0;
      if (choice === 'pass') {
        return `album ${pct}% · pass the groove — wings path stays open for the next listen`;
      }
      const nextWings = (GameProgress?.getState?.()?.wins ?? 0) + 1;
      return `album ${pct}% · keep the groove — ${nextWings} wing${nextWings === 1 ? '' : 's'} in the glass after tonight`;
    }

    function updateMirrorChoiceAlbumUI() {
      const el = document.getElementById('mirrorAlbumPct');
      if (!el) return;
      const { headline, detail } = formatAlbumBreakdownDetail();
      const choice = document.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt')[mirrorChoiceIdx]?.dataset?.choice || 'keep';
      const consequence = mirrorChoiceConsequence(choice);
      el.replaceChildren();
      el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(headline));
      const sub = document.createElement('span');
      sub.className = 'mirror-album-detail';
      sub.textContent = `${detail} · ${consequence}`;
      el.appendChild(sub);
      const hint = document.getElementById('mirrorChoiceHint');
      if (hint) {
        hint.textContent = '↑↓ choose · z or enter to keep the sound';
      }
    }

    function updateAlbumPctIntro() {
      const el = document.getElementById('albumPctIntro');
      if (!el || !GameProgress) return;
      const b = GameProgress.getAlbumBreakdown?.();
      const wings = GameProgress.getState().wins;
      el.textContent = b
        ? `${formatAlbumBreakdown()} · ${wings} wing${wings === 1 ? '' : 's'}`
        : `album completion · ${GameProgress.getAlbumPct()}% · ${wings} wing${wings === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
    }

    function updateEndAlbumDisplay() {
      const el = document.getElementById('albumPctEnd');
      if (!el) return;
      const { headline, detail } = formatAlbumBreakdownDetail();
      el.replaceChildren();
      el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(headline || 'album completion'));
      const sub = document.createElement('span');
      sub.className = 'album-pct-detail';
      sub.textContent = detail || 'store · rhythm · wings';
      el.appendChild(sub);
      el.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function tryUnlockGroove() {
      if (grooveMode || earlyPerfects < GROOVE_EARLY_NEED || slicesEaten > 4) return;
      grooveMode = true;
      improvMeter = Math.min(100, improvMeter + 18);
      document.getElementById('improvFill').style.width = improvMeter + '%';
      document.getElementById('improvLvl').textContent = Math.round(improvMeter);
      showPhaseBrief('groove mode · in the pocket', 2400);
      updateModePill();
    }

    function classifyHit(diff, note = null) {
      let tier = HeavyRuntime.classifyHit(diff, winPerfectMs, winGreatMs, winGoodMs);
      if (note?.echoRich && tier === 'good' && diff <= winGreatMs * 1.12) tier = 'great';
      if (note?.echoOrbBoost && tier === 'good' && diff <= winGreatMs * 1.18) tier = 'great';
      if (note?.echoOrbBoost && tier === 'great' && diff <= winPerfectMs * 1.12) tier = 'perfect';
      return tier;
    }

    const JUDGE_LABEL = { perfect: 'SLICE!', great: 'TASTY!', good: 'NOM!' };

    function updateUI() {
      const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');
      if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = score;
      const slicesEl = document.getElementById('slices');
      if (slicesEl) slicesEl.textContent = slicesEaten;
      const pb = document.getElementById('pizzaBites');
      if (pb) pb.textContent = bonusPizzaEaten;
      const comboEl = document.getElementById('combo');
      if (comboEl) {
        comboEl.textContent = combo > 1 ? combo : '—';
        if (comboEl.parentElement) comboEl.parentElement.style.opacity = combo > 1 ? '1' : '0.45';
      }
    }

    function addSlice(color, note = null) {
      if (note && note.countsTowardGoal === false) return;
      if (!RHYTHM_SONGS[currentSongIdx]?.countsTowardGoal) return;
      slicesEaten++;
      collectedColors.push(color);
      moonOpacity = Math.min(0.95, moonOpacity + 0.12);
      if (slicesEaten === 8) {
        showPhaseBrief('side B.... mirror approaching....', 3000);
        setTimeout(() => showPhaseBrief('you ate the bridge!', 2800), 700);
        bridgeFlashUntil = performance.now() + 1100;
        screenShake = 5.2;
        moonOpacity = Math.min(0.95, moonOpacity + 0.18);
        pauseQuoteCycler(1400);
      }
      if (slicesEaten === 12) {
        showPhaseBrief('theres a mirror at the edge of the world..', 2800);
        document.querySelector('.mirror-watermark')?.classList.add('egg-pulse');
        setTimeout(() => document.querySelector('.mirror-watermark')?.classList.remove('egg-pulse'), 1200);
      }
      updateUI();
      if (slicesEaten >= GOAL_SLICES) markFeastComplete();
    }

    function burst(x, y, color, n = 16) {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const a = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / n + Math.random() * 0.4;
        particles.push({
          x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * (2 + Math.random() * 3),
          vy: Math.sin(a) * (2 + Math.random() * 3) - 1.5,
          life: 1, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 2.5
        });
      }
    }

    function judgeNote(note, judgment) {
      note.hit = true; note.chewing = false; hits++;
      recentMissStreak = 0;
      if (judgment === 'perfect') {
        perfects++;
        recentPerfectStreak++;
        screenShake = 3.2;
        if (slicesEaten < 4) {
          earlyPerfects++;
          tryUnlockGroove();
        }
      } else {
        recentPerfectStreak = 0;
        if (judgment === 'great') greats++;
        else goods++;
      }
      difficultyTier = computeDifficultyTier();
      const scoreAdd = {
        perfect: 220 + improvMeter,
        great: 150 + Math.floor(improvMeter * 0.7),
        good: 90 + Math.floor(improvMeter * 0.4),
      }[judgment] || 90;
      const grooveMult = grooveMode ? GROOVE_SCORE_MULT : 1;
      score += Math.round(scoreAdd * echoScoreMult * grooveMult);
      combo++;
      document.body.classList.toggle('combo-hot', combo >= 8);
      const sticker = document.getElementById('comboSticker');
      if (sticker) sticker.classList.toggle('visible', combo >= 8);
      if (combo === 8) {
        showPhaseBrief('in the pocket');
        echoHudPulseUntil = performance.now() + 1200;
        if (audioCtx) HeavyRuntime.playComboStinger?.(audioCtx, sfxGain, 8);
      } else if (combo === 16) {
        showPhaseBrief('full swing');
        screenShake = Math.min(5, screenShake + 2);
        document.body.classList.add('combo-milestone');
        setTimeout(() => document.body.classList.remove('combo-milestone'), 280);
        if (audioCtx) HeavyRuntime.playComboStinger?.(audioCtx, sfxGain, 16);
      } else if (combo === 32) {
        showPhaseBrief('devouring the side');
        screenShake = Math.min(6, screenShake + 3);
        document.body.classList.add('combo-milestone');
        setTimeout(() => document.body.classList.remove('combo-milestone'), 360);
        if (audioCtx) HeavyRuntime.playComboStinger?.(audioCtx, sfxGain, 32);
      } else if (combo === 42) handleEggUnlock(EasterEggs?.onRhythmCombo?.(42), { blip: false });
      if (!scoreEggFired && score >= 2222) {
        scoreEggFired = true;
        handleEggUnlock(EasterEggs?.onRhythmScore?.(score), { blip: false });
      }
      playLaneHit(note.lane, judgment);
      showJudgment(JUDGE_LABEL[judgment] || 'NOM!', judgment);
      flashKey(note.lane, judgment);
      const ring = HeavyRuntime.judgmentBurst(particles, laneCenter(note.lane), hitY, note.color, judgment);
      hitFlashes.push({
        lane: note.lane, life: 1, tier: judgment,
        maxR: laneW * (ring + 0.2),
      });
      if (hitFlashes.length > 16) hitFlashes.splice(0, hitFlashes.length - 16);
      if (judgment === 'perfect') {
        floatNotes.push({
          x: laneCenter(note.lane), y: hitY - 30, life: 1.2,
          glyph: '∴', color: '#f8f4ff',
        });
      }
      addSlice(note.color, note);
      updateUI();
    }

    function missNote(note) {
      if (note.missed) return;
      note.missed = true; misses++; combo = 0;
      recentMissStreak++;
      recentPerfectStreak = 0;
      difficultyTier = computeDifficultyTier();
      document.body.classList.remove('combo-hot');
      document.getElementById('comboSticker')?.classList.remove('visible');
      if (audioCtx) HeavyRuntime.playMissScratch(audioCtx, sfxGain);
      showJudgment('faded into static', 'miss'); flashKey(note.lane, 'miss');
      desaturateUntil = performance.now() + 450;
      improvMeter = Math.max(0, improvMeter - 8);
      document.getElementById('improvFill').style.width = improvMeter + '%';
      document.getElementById('improvLvl').textContent = Math.round(improvMeter);
      updateUI();
    }

    function tryHit(lane) {
      if (!running) return;
      const now = performance.now() - songStart;
      if (tryHitBonusPizza(lane, now)) return;
      playImprov(lane);
      let best = null, bestDiff = Infinity;
      for (const n of activeNotes) {
        if (n.lane !== lane || n.hit || n.missed || n.chewing) continue;
        if (now < n.time - HIT_LEAD_MS) continue;
        const diff = Math.abs(now - n.time);
        if (diff < winGoodMs && diff < bestDiff) { best = n; bestDiff = diff; }
      }
      if (!best) return;
      if (best.type === 'hold') {
        best.hit = true; best.chewing = true; best.chewingStart = now;
        showJudgment('chew...', 'good'); flashKey(lane, '');
      } else {
        const tier = classifyHit(bestDiff, best);
        if (tier) judgeNote(best, tier);
      }
    }

    function releaseLane(lane) {
      for (const n of activeNotes) {
        if (n.lane !== lane || !n.chewing) continue;
        const now = performance.now() - songStart;
        if (now - n.chewingStart >= n.duration * 0.5) judgeNote(n, 'good');
        else missNote(n);
      }
    }

    function checkMisses(now) {
      for (const n of activeNotes) {
        if (n.missed) continue;
        if (n.chewing) {
          const anchor = n.chewingStart ?? n.time;
          if (now - anchor > n.duration + winGoodMs * 2) missNote(n);
          continue;
        }
        if (!n.hit && !n.chewing) {
          const late = now - n.time;
          if (late > winGoodMs + chillAutoMs) missNote(n);
          else if (late > winGoodMs && chillAutoMs > 0 && n.type !== 'hold') {
            judgeNote(n, 'good');
          }
        }
      }
      activeNotes = activeNotes.filter((n) => !n.missed && (n.chewing || now - n.time < approachTime + 800));
      cullActiveNotes();
    }

    function clearAftermathReturnTimer() {
      if (aftermathReturnTimer) {
        clearTimeout(aftermathReturnTimer);
        aftermathReturnTimer = null;
      }
    }

    function clearAftermathDialogueTimer() {
      if (aftermathDialogueTimer) {
        clearTimeout(aftermathDialogueTimer);
        aftermathDialogueTimer = null;
      }
    }

    function scheduleAftermathReturn(delayMs = 4500) {
      clearAftermathReturnTimer();
      const hint = document.getElementById('endReturnHint');
      if (hint) hint.textContent = 'walking back to the store...';
      aftermathReturnTimer = setTimeout(() => enterAftermathStore(), delayMs);
    }

    function showRhythmEndScreen({ won }) {
      const pct = GameProgress?.getAlbumPct?.() ?? 0;
      const tier = GameProgress?.getAftermathTier?.() || 'tasty';
      const tierLabels = {
        wings: 'wings in the glass',
        groove: 'groove remembered',
        tasty: 'still hungry',
        static: 'needle paused',
      };

      if (won) {
        const variant = GameProgress?.getEndVariant();
        document.getElementById('endTitle').textContent = variant?.title || 'when its real';
        const choice = GameProgress?.getLastRun()?.grooveChoice || mirrorGrooveChoice;
        const passLine = choice === 'pass'
          ? 'you passed the groove on — the store will hum it for the next walker.'
          : (variant?.subtitle || 'no words are needed.... theres a mirror at the edge of the world..');
        document.getElementById('endSubtitle').textContent = passLine;
        const albumEcho = choice === 'pass'
          ? `album ${pct}% · groove passed on`
          : `album ${pct}% · groove kept`;
        const extra = variant?.extra ? ` · ${variant.extra}` : '';
        const pizzaLine = bonusPizzaEaten > 0 ? ` · ${bonusPizzaEaten} pizza bite${bonusPizzaEaten === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : '';
        const breakdown = formatAlbumBreakdown();
        document.getElementById('endSummary').textContent =
          `score ${score} · ${perfects} SLICE! · improv ${Math.round(improvMeter)}% · ${albumEcho}${pizzaLine}${extra}`
          + (breakdown ? ` · ${breakdown}` : '');
      } else {
        document.getElementById('endTitle').textContent = 'keep jamming';
        document.getElementById('endSubtitle').textContent =
          tier === 'static'
            ? 'we still ate some sounds together.... that counts for something'
            : 'the store stays open late';
        const breakdown = formatAlbumBreakdown();
        document.getElementById('endSummary').textContent =
          `${slicesEaten}/${GOAL_SLICES} slices · score ${score}`
          + (breakdown ? ` · ${breakdown}` : ` · album ${pct}%`);
      }
      const tierVibes = {
        wings: 'you left with wings in the glass',
        groove: 'you left with groove remembered',
        tasty: 'you left still hungry — that is okay',
        static: 'you left with some sounds still in your pockets — cozy, not failure',
      };
      document.getElementById('endReturnHint').textContent =
        `${tierLabels[tier] || tier} · ${tierVibes[tier] || 'the floor remembers your groove'}`;
      document.getElementById('endOverlay').classList.remove('hidden');
      updateEndAlbumDisplay();
      scheduleAftermathReturn(won ? 4800 : 3600);
    }

    function closeMirrorChoice() {
      mirrorChoiceOpen = false;
      document.getElementById('mirrorChoice')?.classList.remove('visible');
      if (mirrorChoiceTimer) { clearTimeout(mirrorChoiceTimer); mirrorChoiceTimer = null; }
    }

    function resetMirrorFlow() {
      mirrorChoiceConfirming = false;
      mirrorChoiceLocked = false;
      finishWinStarted = false;
      closeMirrorChoice();
      if (finishWinTimer) { clearTimeout(finishWinTimer); finishWinTimer = null; }
      if (mirrorOpenTimer) { clearTimeout(mirrorOpenTimer); mirrorOpenTimer = null; }
    }

    function flashMirrorSarah(choice) {
      const el = document.getElementById('mirrorSarahFlash');
      const c = document.getElementById('mirrorSarahCanvas');
      if (!el || !c) return;
      const px = c.getContext('2d');
      px.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
      const cx = c.width / 2;
      const cy = c.height / 2;
      if (!HeavyDialogueArt.drawGrokPortrait(px, cx, cy, 84, 0)) {
        HeavyDialogueArt.drawPixelNinjawhee(px, cx, cy, 4.2, 'talk', 0);
      }
      el.classList.remove('hidden', 'reflecting', 'keep', 'pass');
      el.classList.add('visible', choice === 'pass' ? 'pass' : 'keep');
      void el.offsetWidth;
      el.classList.add('reflecting');
      clearTimeout(flashMirrorSarah._t);
      flashMirrorSarah._t = setTimeout(() => {
        el.classList.remove('visible', 'reflecting', 'keep', 'pass');
        el.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 1100);
    }

    function confirmMirrorChoice() {
      if (mirrorChoiceConfirming || mirrorChoiceLocked || !mirrorChoiceOpen) return;
      mirrorChoiceConfirming = true;
      mirrorChoiceLocked = true;
      if (mirrorChoiceTimer) { clearTimeout(mirrorChoiceTimer); mirrorChoiceTimer = null; }
      const opts = document.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt');
      mirrorGrooveChoice = opts[mirrorChoiceIdx]?.dataset?.choice || 'keep';
      const mc = document.getElementById('mirrorChoice');
      mc?.classList.remove('flash-keep', 'flash-pass');
      mc?.classList.add(mirrorGrooveChoice === 'keep' ? 'flash-keep' : 'flash-pass');
      updateMirrorChoiceAlbumUI();
      flashMirrorSarah(mirrorGrooveChoice);
      showPhaseBrief(mirrorChoiceConsequence(mirrorGrooveChoice), 2200);
      document.getElementById('mirrorChoiceHint')?.classList.remove('pulse');
      closeMirrorChoice();
      GameProgress?.recordRun({
        slices: slicesEaten, perfects, score, won: true,
        improv: Math.round(improvMeter), misses,
        grooveChoice: mirrorGrooveChoice,
      });
      playConfirmBlip();
      if (mirrorGrooveChoice === 'keep') {
        ripples.push({ lane: 2, y: H * 0.38, life: 1, maxR: laneW * 2.2, color: '#ffaa66' });
        ripples.push({ lane: 1, y: H * 0.42, life: 1, maxR: laneW * 1.6, color: '#c9a84c' });
      } else {
        ripples.push({ lane: 2, y: H * 0.38, life: 1, maxR: laneW * 2.2, color: '#7b5ea7' });
        ripples.push({ lane: 3, y: H * 0.35, life: 1, maxR: laneW * 1.8, color: '#9b7ec8' });
      }
      document.getElementById('phaseLabel').textContent = 'metamorphosis';
      document.getElementById('phaseLabel').classList.add('visible');
      if (finishWinTimer) clearTimeout(finishWinTimer);
      finishWinTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        showRhythmEndScreen({ won: true });
        document.getElementById('phaseLabel').classList.remove('visible');
        finishWinTimer = null;
      }, 1800);
    }

    function openMirrorChoice() {
      mirrorChoiceConfirming = false;
      mirrorChoiceLocked = false;
      closeMirrorChoice();
      mirrorChoiceOpen = true;
      mirrorChoiceIdx = 0;
      document.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt').forEach((el, i) => {
        el.classList.toggle('selected', i === 0);
      });
      document.getElementById('mirrorChoice')?.classList.add('visible');
      updateMirrorChoiceAlbumUI();
      document.getElementById('mirrorChoiceHint')?.classList.add('pulse');
    }

    function finishWin() {
      if (finishWinStarted) return;
      finishWinStarted = true;
      rhythmHandoffGen++;
      running = false;
      document.body.classList.remove('rhythm-active', 'combo-hot');
      rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
      cleanupRhythmSession();
      audioBus?.setMode('idle');
      stopQuoteCycler();
      playConfirmBlip();
      document.getElementById('phaseLabel').textContent = 'feast complete';
      document.getElementById('phaseLabel').classList.add('visible');
      if (mirrorOpenTimer) clearTimeout(mirrorOpenTimer);
      mirrorOpenTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        mirrorOpenTimer = null;
        document.getElementById('phaseLabel').classList.remove('visible');
        openMirrorChoice();
      }, 1400);
    }

    function endSong() {
      if (slicesEaten >= GOAL_SLICES) { finishWin(); return; }
      rhythmHandoffGen++;
      running = false;
      document.body.classList.remove('rhythm-active');
      closeMirrorChoice();
      if (finishWinTimer) { clearTimeout(finishWinTimer); finishWinTimer = null; }
      rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
      cleanupRhythmSession();
      audioBus?.setMode('idle');
      stopQuoteCycler();
      GameProgress?.recordRun({
        slices: slicesEaten, perfects, score, won: false,
        improv: Math.round(improvMeter), misses,
      });
      document.body.classList.add('weak-run-sting');
      showPhaseBrief('we still ate some sounds together....', 2200);
      for (let lane = 0; lane < 4; lane++) {
        burst(laneCenter(lane), hitY, '#c9a84c', 10);
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.body.classList.remove('weak-run-sting');
        showRhythmEndScreen({ won: false });
      }, 520);
    }

    function drawShelves() {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.14 + improvMeter * 0.001;
      ctx.font = '7px "Space Mono"';
      for (let row = 0; row < 2; row++) {
        const y = H * 0.05 + row * 22;
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(201,168,76,0.35)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(laneX0, y); ctx.lineTo(laneX0 + laneW * 4, y); ctx.stroke();
        for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
          const x = laneX0 + i * (laneW * 4 / 14);
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(201,168,76,${0.12 + improvMeter * 0.002})`;
          ctx.fillRect(x, y - 14, 10, 14);
          if (i % 3 === 0 && row === 0) {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(232,224,240,0.22)';
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(x + 5, y - 4);
            ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);
            ctx.fillText(SHELF_SPINES[i % SHELF_SPINES.length].slice(0, 10), 0, 0);
            ctx.restore();
          }
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    function drawCathedralArch() {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.07 + improvMeter * 0.0008;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(201,168,76,0.5)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      const archW = laneW * 4 * 0.55;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx - archW, laneTopY - 8);
      ctx.lineTo(cx - archW, laneTopY - 48);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, laneTopY - 88, cx + archW, laneTopY - 48);
      ctx.lineTo(cx + archW, laneTopY - 8);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.font = '11px serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(232,224,240,0.25)';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('𓅰', cx, laneTopY - 52);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    function drawRhythmAmbience() {
      const pulse = 0.5 + Math.sin(time * 0.04) * 0.2 + improvMeter * 0.003;
      for (let lane = 0; lane < 4; lane++) {
        const lx = laneCenter(lane);
        const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(lx, hitY + 28, 4, lx, hitY + 28, laneW * 0.55);
        const col = LANES[lane].color;
        g.addColorStop(0, col + '22');
        g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent');
        ctx.fillStyle = g;
        ctx.fillRect(lx - laneW * 0.5, hitY - 20, laneW, 80);
      }
      const pizzaX = laneX0 - laneW * 0.35;
      const pizzaY = H * 0.22;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.12 + pulse * 0.08;
      const pg = ctx.createRadialGradient(pizzaX, pizzaY, 8, pizzaX, pizzaY, 48);
      pg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(232,160,76,0.55)');
      pg.addColorStop(1, 'transparent');
      ctx.fillStyle = pg;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(pizzaX, pizzaY, 46, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35 + pulse * 0.15;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e8a04c';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(pizzaX, pizzaY - 14);
      ctx.arc(pizzaX, pizzaY, 16, -Math.PI * 0.75, Math.PI * 0.55);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
      bgVinylSpin.forEach((phase, i) => {
        const vx = laneX0 + laneW * 4 + laneW * (0.15 + i * 0.12);
        const vy = H * (0.14 + i * 0.11);
        const spin = time * 0.018 + phase;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.1 + (i === 0 ? pulse * 0.06 : 0.04);
        ctx.translate(vx, vy);
        ctx.rotate(spin);
        PixelGfx.drawVinylRecord(ctx, 0, 0, 14 + i * 2, LANES[i].color, spin * 0.3, '');
        ctx.restore();
      });
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.08 + improvMeter * 0.001;
      ctx.font = '6px "Space Mono"';
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(201,168,76,0.45)';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('jazz records', cx, laneTopY - 18);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    function drawBackground() {
      for (let y = 0; y < H; y += 6) {
        const t = y / H;
        ctx.fillStyle = t < 0.4 ? '#1c1230' : t < 0.7 ? '#140e20' : '#0a0812';
        ctx.fillRect(0, y, W, 6);
      }
      drawCathedralArch();
      drawShelves();
      drawRhythmAmbience();
      for (const s of stars) {
        const a = 0.2 + Math.sin(time * s.sp + s.tw) * 0.15;
        PixelGfx.fillPixel(ctx, s.x, s.y, s.r, `rgba(232,224,240,${a})`);
      }
      const moonX = laneX0 + laneW * 4 - 30, moonY = H * 0.1;
      ctx.globalAlpha = moonOpacity;
      PixelGfx.drawPixelMoon(ctx, moonX, moonY, 16 + improvMeter * 0.06);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      PixelGfx.drawScanlines(ctx, W, H, 0.035);
    }

    function drawLanes() {
      HeavyRuntime.drawAlignedPlayfield(ctx, {
        laneX0, laneW, laneTopY, hitY, cx,
        goodMs: winGoodMs, greatMs: winGreatMs, perfectMs: winPerfectMs,
        approachTime,
      }, LANES, time);
    }

    function drawRipples() {
      for (let i = ripples.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const r = ripples[i];
        r.life -= 0.035;
        if (r.life <= 0) { ripples.splice(i, 1); continue; }
        const radius = r.maxR * (1 - r.life);
        const stroke = r.color || LANES[r.lane].color;
        const alpha = Math.floor(r.life * 200).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
        PixelGfx.fillPixelDisk(ctx, laneCenter(r.lane), r.y, radius, 4, stroke + alpha);
      }
    }

    function drawHitFlashes() {
      for (let i = hitFlashes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const f = hitFlashes[i];
        f.life -= f.tier === 'perfect' ? 0.06 : 0.045;
        if (f.life <= 0) { hitFlashes.splice(i, 1); continue; }
        const col = f.tier === 'perfect' ? '#f8f4ff' : f.tier === 'great' ? '#4a8f7a' : '#c9a84c';
        const x = laneCenter(f.lane);
        const h = 8 * f.life;
        PixelGfx.fillPixelRect(ctx, x - laneW * 0.3, hitY - h, laneW * 0.6, h * 2, 4, col + '55');
      }
    }

    function drawFloatNotes() {
      for (let i = floatNotes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const n = floatNotes[i];
        n.y -= 1.2; n.life -= 0.018;
        if (n.life <= 0) { floatNotes.splice(i, 1); continue; }
        ctx.globalAlpha = n.life * 0.7;
        ctx.font = '18px serif'; ctx.fillStyle = n.color;
        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(n.glyph, n.x, n.y);
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }

    function drawVinylNote(n, now) {
      const fallProgress = noteFallProgress(n, now);
      const y = noteY(n, now);
      const x = laneCenter(n.lane);
      const r = 30;
      const diff = Math.abs(now - n.time);
      const tier = classifyHit(diff, n);
      const inWindow = now >= n.time - HIT_LEAD_MS && tier;
      const beatPhase = (performance.now() - songStart) / beatMs;
      const spin = beatPhase * (Math.PI * 2 / 8) + n.lane * 0.45;

      if (n.chewing) {
        const chewT = Math.min(1, (now - n.chewingStart) / n.duration);
        PixelGfx.fillPixelRect(ctx, x - laneW * 0.28, hitY - 8, laneW * 0.56 * chewT, 12, 4, n.color);
      }

      const sliceAlpha = fallProgress < 0.35 ? 0.55 + fallProgress * 1.2 : 1;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = sliceAlpha;
      PixelGfx.setupPixelCtx(ctx);
      PixelGfx.drawVinylRecord(ctx, x, y, r, n.color, spin, n.label);

      if (n.type === 'hold') {
        ctx.font = '6px "Press Start 2P", monospace';
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f8f4e8';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        ctx.fillText('CHEW', x, y + r * 0.55);
      }
      if (inWindow && !n.hit) {
        const ringColor = tier === 'perfect' ? '#f8f4ff' : tier === 'great' ? '#4a8f7a' : '#c9a84c';
        PixelGfx.fillPixelRect(ctx, x - r - 6, y - r - 6, r * 2 + 12, 4, 4, ringColor);
        PixelGfx.fillPixelRect(ctx, x - r - 6, y + r + 2, r * 2 + 12, 4, 4, ringColor);
      }
      ctx.restore();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      if (inWindow && !n.hit) {
        const dotColor = tier === 'perfect' ? '#f8f4ff' : tier === 'great' ? '#4a8f7a' : n.color;
        PixelGfx.fillPixelDisk(ctx, x, hitY, 6 + (Math.sin(time * 0.2) > 0 ? 2 : 0), 4, dotColor + 'cc');
      }
    }

    function loop() {
      if (!running) return;
      ctx.save();
      if (screenShake > 0.12) {
        ctx.translate(
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * screenShake,
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * screenShake * 0.65
        );
        screenShake *= 0.78;
      } else screenShake = 0;
      time++;
      const now = performance.now() - songStart;
      spawnNotes(now);
      maybeSpawnBonusPizza(now);
      checkMisses(now);
      checkBonusPizzaMisses(now);
      updateHint(now);
      checkPhases(now);
      injectLivePressure(now);
      const progBoost = slicesEaten >= 8 ? 1.22 : grooveMode ? 1.08 : 1;
      document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width =
        Math.min(100, (now / (songBeats * beatMs)) * 100 * progBoost) + '%';

      drawBackground();
      if (bridgeFlashUntil > performance.now()) {
        const bf = (bridgeFlashUntil - performance.now()) / 1100;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = bf * 0.34;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#e8d48c';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      drawLanes();
      if (grooveMode) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.06 + Math.sin(time * 0.08) * 0.04;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#c9a84c';
        ctx.fillRect(laneX0, laneTopY, laneW * 4, hitY - laneTopY + 20);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      VinylEchoBridge?.drawRhythmGhosts(ctx, laneX0, laneW, hitY, slicesEaten, time);
      drawRipples(); drawHitFlashes();
      for (const n of activeNotes) {
        if (!n.missed && (!n.hit || n.chewing)) drawVinylNote(n, now);
      }
      for (const p of bonusPizzas) {
        if (!p.hit && !p.missed) drawBonusPizza(p, now);
      }
      drawFloatNotes();
      if (echoSeed?.vinyls?.length) {
        const pulse = echoHudPulseUntil > performance.now();
        VinylEchoBridge?.drawRhythmEchoOrbs?.(ctx, 12, 52, echoSeed.vinyls.length, time, pulse);
      }
      if (particles.length > 72) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 72);
      for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const p = particles[i];
        p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.vy += 0.025; p.life -= 0.028;
        if (p.life <= 0) { particles.splice(i, 1); continue; }
        ctx.globalAlpha = p.life; ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
        const sz = Math.max(2, Math.floor(p.size * p.life));
        if (p.tier) PixelGfx.fillPixel(ctx, p.x, p.y, sz, p.color);
        else { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, sz, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (now > songBeats * beatMs + 2000) {
        ctx.restore();
        if (feastComplete && !finishWinStarted) {
          encoreAdvanceCount++;
          advanceToNextSong();
          requestAnimationFrame(loop);
          return;
        }
        if (!finishWinStarted) endSong();
        return;
      }
      ctx.restore();
      requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    }

    async function runCountdown() {
      const el = document.getElementById('countdown');
      for (const txt of ['3', '2', '1', '♫']) {
        el.textContent = txt; el.classList.add('show');
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, txt === '♫' ? 350 : 650));
        el.classList.remove('show');
      }
    }

    function initPalette() {
      document.getElementById('goal').textContent = GOAL_SLICES;
      const el = document.getElementById('palette');
      el.innerHTML = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < GOAL_SLICES; i++) {
        const d = document.createElement('div');
        d.className = 'color-dot'; d.id = `dot-${i}`;
        el.appendChild(d);
      }
    }

    async function startGame() {
      if (gameStarting || running) return;
      gameStarting = true;
      running = false;
      rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
      cleanupRhythmSession();
      stopQuoteCycler();
      try {
      initAudio();
      await audioBus?.resume();
      audioBus?.setMode('rhythm');
      document.body.classList.add('rhythm-active');
      echoSeed = window.VinylEchoBridge?.getSeed() || null;
      VinylEchoBridge?.resetSession?.();
      echoGoodMs = GOOD_MS + (echoSeed?.nomBiasMs || 0);
      echoScoreMult = echoSeed?.multi || 1;
      mirrorGrooveChoice = 'keep';
      resetMirrorFlow();
      grooveMode = false;
      earlyPerfects = 0;
      syncTimingWindows();
      if (echoSeed?.vinyls?.length) {
        const rich = echoSeed.vinyls.length > 2 ? ' · TASTY boost on bites' : '';
        showPhaseBrief(`echo orbs → richer slices · ${echoSeed.vinyls.length} orb${echoSeed.vinyls.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}${rich}`, 2800);
      }
      if (GameProgress?.isChill()) {
        setTimeout(() => showPhaseBrief('chill mode · wide bite · auto-NOM', 2200), 600);
      } else if (document.body.classList.contains('grace-active')) {
        setTimeout(() => showPhaseBrief('wide bite windows · take your time', 2200), 600);
      }
      score = 0; combo = 0; slicesEaten = 0;
      hits = 0; misses = 0; perfects = 0; greats = 0; goods = 0;
      collectedColors = []; particles = []; ripples = []; floatNotes = []; hitFlashes = [];
      improvMeter = 0; improvStep = [0, 0, 0, 0];
      moonOpacity = 0.3;
      scoreEggFired = false;
      initPalette(); updateUI();
      document.getElementById('introOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('endOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width = '0%';
      document.getElementById('improvFill').style.width = '0%';
      document.getElementById('improvLvl').textContent = '0';
      rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
      stopQuoteCycler();
      await runCountdown();
      buildChart();
      activeNotes = [];
      rhythmLoop?.start();
      songStart = performance.now();
      running = true; loop();
      } finally {
        gameStarting = false;
      }
    }

    document.getElementById('startBtn').addEventListener('click', startGame);
    document.getElementById('returnStoreBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      clearAftermathReturnTimer();
      enterAftermathStore();
    });
    document.getElementById('restartBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      clearAftermathReturnTimer();
      document.getElementById('endOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
      dialogue.skipIntro = true;
      startGame();
    });

    document.querySelector('.mirror-watermark')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const el = document.querySelector('.mirror-watermark');
      el?.classList.add('egg-pulse');
      setTimeout(() => el?.classList.remove('egg-pulse'), 300);
      const glyph = EasterEggs?.onMirrorTap?.();
      if (glyph) handleEggUnlock(glyph);
      else if (EasterEggs?.has?.('mirror_glyph')) showPhaseBrief('∴𓅰', 600);
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.triggerEchoRipple?.();
      } else {
        showPhaseBrief('∴ echo ripple', 900);
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('mirrorChoice')?.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt').forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!mirrorChoiceOpen) return;
        mirrorChoiceIdx = [...el.parentElement.children].indexOf(el);
        el.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt').forEach((o, i) => {
          o.classList.toggle('selected', i === mirrorChoiceIdx);
        });
        updateMirrorChoiceAlbumUI();
      });
    });

    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (mirrorChoiceOpen) {
        if (e.code === 'ArrowUp' || e.code === 'ArrowDown') {
          e.preventDefault();
          const opts = document.querySelectorAll('.mirror-opt');
          mirrorChoiceIdx = (mirrorChoiceIdx + (e.code === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1) + opts.length) % opts.length;
          opts.forEach((o, i) => o.classList.toggle('selected', i === mirrorChoiceIdx));
          updateMirrorChoiceAlbumUI();
        }
        if (['KeyZ', 'Enter', 'Space'].includes(e.code)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          confirmMirrorChoice();
        }
        return;
      }
      if (!dialogue.active && !running && !mirrorChoiceOpen) {
        const egg = EasterEggs?.onKey?.(e.code, eggPhase());
        if (egg) {
          handleEggUnlock(egg, { dialogueNode: 'pizza' });
          if (audioCtx && egg.id === 'dfjk') {
            audioBus?.resume();
            const ping = audioCtx.createOscillator();
            const g = audioCtx.createGain();
            ping.type = 'triangle';
            ping.frequency.value = 523.25;
            g.gain.value = 0.1;
            ping.connect(g).connect(sfxGain || masterGain);
            const t = audioCtx.currentTime;
            ping.start(t);
            g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.35);
            ping.stop(t + 0.38);
          }
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.active || document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) return;
      const lane = KEY_MAP[e.code];
      if (lane !== undefined) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!running) return;
        if (!held[lane]) {
          held[lane] = true;
          document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${lane}"]`).classList.add('pressed');
          if (audioCtx) {
            audioBus?.resume();
            duckRhythmSfx(0.1);
            HeavyRuntime.playKeyTap(audioCtx, sfxGain, lane, LANES[lane].pitch);
          }
          tryHit(lane);
        }

        return;
      }
      const introOpen = !document.getElementById('introOverlay').classList.contains('hidden');
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !running && introOpen) startGame();
      if (e.code === 'KeyZ' && running && feastComplete && !finishWinStarted) {
        e.preventDefault();
        finishWin();
        return;
      }
      if (e.code === 'Escape' && running) {
        if (feastComplete && slicesEaten >= GOAL_SLICES) finishWin();
        else endSong();
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      const lane = KEY_MAP[e.code];
      if (lane === undefined) return;
      held[lane] = false;

      document.querySelector(`.key-cap[data-lane="${lane}"]`).classList.remove('pressed');
      releaseLane(lane);
    });

    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    resize(); initPalette(); updateUI();
