    const dialogue = {
      active: false,
      skipIntro: false,
      forest: 'intro',
      mode: 'cinematic',
      portrait: 'ninjawhee',
      nodeId: 'open',
      lineIdx: 0,
      charIdx: 0,
      typing: false,
      waiting: false,
      choiceIdx: 0,
      talkFrame: false,
      emotion: 'idle',
      bob: 0,
      sceneT: 0,
      typeTimer: 0,
      fullLine: '',
      _afterYou: null,
    };

    const pixelCanvas = document.getElementById('pixelSprite');
    const pixelCtx = pixelCanvas.getContext('2d');
    const bgCanvas = document.getElementById('dialogueBgCanvas');
    const bgCtx = bgCanvas.getContext('2d');

    function resizeDialogueCanvas() {
      bgCanvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      bgCanvas.height = window.innerHeight;
    }
    resizeDialogueCanvas();
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      resizeDialogueCanvas();
      JazzStoreOverworld.resize();
    });
    const dLine = document.getElementById('dialogueLine');
    const dSpeaker = document.getElementById('dialogueSpeaker');
    const dCursor = document.getElementById('dialogueCursor');
    const dChoices = document.getElementById('dialogueChoices');

    function playDialogueBlip() {
      initAudio();
      if (!audioCtx || audioCtx.state === 'suspended') return;
      HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(
        audioCtx, masterGain, 'talk',
        HeavyDialogueArt.blipPitch(dSpeaker.textContent)
      );
      dialogue.talkFrame = !dialogue.talkFrame;
    }

    function playChoiceBlip() {
      initAudio();
      if (!audioCtx) return;
      HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, masterGain, 'choice');
    }

    function playConfirmBlip() {
      initAudio();
      if (!audioCtx) return;
      HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, masterGain, 'confirm');
    }

    const dScene = document.getElementById('dialogueScene');
    const spriteLabel = document.querySelector('.sprite-label');

    function updatePortraitLabel() {
      const labels = {
        ninjawhee: 'sarah א‎ · @ninjawhee',
        stranger: 'street visitor',
        orph: '@orphcorp · mutual',
        simon: '@simonsarris · mutual',
        honey: '@honeyNonABG · mutual',
        bird: 'little store bird · ♪',
      };
      spriteLabel.textContent = labels[dialogue.portrait] || dialogue.portrait;
    }

    function drawDialogueFrame() {
      dialogue.sceneT++;
      const bob = Math.sin(dialogue.bob) * 3;
      dialogue.bob += 0.05;
      const frame = dialogue.typing && dialogue.talkFrame ? 'talk' : dialogue.emotion;
      if (dialogue.mode === 'cinematic') {
        HeavyDialogueArt.drawDialogueSceneBg(bgCtx, bgCanvas.width, bgCanvas.height, dialogue.sceneT);
      }
      pixelCtx.clearRect(0, 0, pixelCanvas.width, pixelCanvas.height);
      const cx = pixelCanvas.width / 2;
      const cy = pixelCanvas.height / 2 + 10;
      if (dialogue.portrait === 'ninjawhee') {
        const size = dialogue.mode === 'overworld' ? 140 : Math.min(400, pixelCanvas.width - 12);
        if (!HeavyDialogueArt.drawGrokPortrait(pixelCtx, cx, cy, size, bob)) {
          HeavyDialogueArt.drawPixelNinjawhee(pixelCtx, cx, cy, 5, frame, bob);
        }
      } else if (dialogue.portrait === 'bird' && window.PixelGfx?.drawPixelBird) {
        PixelGfx.setupPixelCtx(pixelCtx);
        PixelGfx.drawPixelBird(pixelCtx, cx, cy, dialogue.sceneT, 'scared');
      } else {
        HeavyDialogueArt.drawNpcPortrait(pixelCtx, cx, cy, 5, dialogue.portrait, bob);
      }
    }

    function spriteLoop() {
      if (!dialogue.active) return;
      drawDialogueFrame();
      requestAnimationFrame(spriteLoop);
    }



    function enrichDialogueNode(node) {
      if (!node) return node;
      if (dialogue.forest === 'aftermath' && ['wings', 'groove', 'tasty', 'static'].includes(dialogue.nodeId) && node.lines) {
        const vibe = {
          wings: 'you left with wings vibes today. the glass is warm.',
          groove: 'you left with groove vibes today. honest timing.',
          tasty: 'you left with tasty vibes today. still hungry is fine.',
          static: 'you left with static vibes today. the door stayed open for you.',
        }[dialogue.nodeId];
        let lines = node.lines;
        if (vibe && !lines[0]?.includes('vibes today')) {
          lines = [vibe, ...lines];
        }
        if (GameProgress?.isFindQuestComplete?.()
            && !lines.some((l) => l.includes('nine traces'))) {
          lines = [
            ...lines,
            'you noticed what they noticed.... all nine traces. the shelves feel thicker tonight.',
          ];
        }
        if (lines !== node.lines) return { ...node, lines };
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'aftermath' && dialogue.nodeId === 'aftermath_hub' && node.lines
          && GameProgress?.isFindQuestComplete?.()
          && !node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('nine traces'))) {
        return {
          ...node,
          lines: [
            'the store is yours tonight.',
            'you found every storm trace and breadcrumb and heartbeat.... they see you now.',
          ],
        };
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'intro' && dialogue.nodeId === 'meet' && node.choices && GameProgress?.hasSecret?.('dfjk')) {
        return {
          ...node,
          choices: [
            ...node.choices,
            { text: 'pizza in the groove?', next: 'secrets_pizza' },
          ],
        };
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'aftermath' && dialogue.nodeId === 'aftermath_hub' && node.choices) {
        const tier = GameProgress?.getAftermathTier() || 'tasty';
        const extra = [];
        if (tier === 'wings') extra.push({ text: 'what did the mirror show?', next: 'mirror_show' });
        if (tier === 'groove') extra.push({ text: 'groove notes?', next: 'groove_notes' });
        if (tier === 'tasty') extra.push({ text: 'timing tips?', next: 'tasty_tips' });
        if (tier === 'static') extra.push({ text: 'chill mode help?', next: 'static_chill' });
        extra.push({ text: 'talk to the mutuals', next: 'mutuals_hint' });
        if ((GameProgress?.getSecretCount?.() || 0) >= 3) {
          extra.push({ text: 'hidden shelf?', next: 'secrets_vault' });
        }
        return { ...node, choices: [...node.choices, ...extra] };
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'return' && dialogue.nodeId === 'return_albums' && node.lines) {
        const heard = GameProgress?.getState()?.vinyls?.length || 0;
        if (heard >= 2) {
          return {
            ...node,
            lines: [...node.lines, 'you really got it... just like when we used to listen here together'],
          };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'return' && dialogue.nodeId === 'open' && node.lines) {
        const echoes = window.VinylEchoBridge?.getVinylListenCount?.() || 0;
        if (echoes < 1 && !node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('echo'))) {
          return {
            ...node,
            lines: [
              ...node.lines,
              'spin a shelf preview first.... echoes make richer bites when you drop the needle.',
            ],
          };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'return' && dialogue.nodeId === 'return_setup' && node.lines
          && (GameProgress?.getState?.()?.runs ?? 0) >= 1) {
        if (!node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('D · F · J · K'))) {
          return {
            ...node,
            lines: [
              ...node.lines,
              'when the store is quiet.... type D · F · J · K in order.',
            ],
          };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'intro' && dialogue.nodeId === 'send_explore' && node.lines) {
        const echoes = window.VinylEchoBridge?.getVinylListenCount?.() || 0;
        if (echoes < 1 && !node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('echo'))) {
          return {
            ...node,
            lines: [
              node.lines[0],
              'press Z on a spinning vinyl — each preview becomes an echo for the needle.',
              ...node.lines.slice(1),
            ],
          };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'return' && dialogue.nodeId === 'return_remember' && node.choices && GameProgress?.getUnlockTier() >= 4) {
        return {
          ...node,
          choices: [
            ...node.choices,
            { text: 'whole album ready?', next: 'return_full_album' },
          ],
        };
      }
      if (['orph', 'simon', 'honey'].includes(dialogue.forest)
          && dialogue.nodeId?.startsWith('aftermath_') && node.lines) {
        const choice = GameProgress?.getLastRun()?.grooveChoice;
        const extra = choice === 'pass'
          ? 'you passed the groove on — the floor still hums it for the next walker.'
          : choice === 'keep'
            ? 'you kept that groove warm — i felt it in the stacks.'
            : null;
        if (extra && !node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('groove'))) {
          return { ...node, lines: [...node.lines, extra] };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'return' && dialogue.nodeId === 'return_nervous' && node.choices) {
        const extra = [];
        if (GameProgress?.isChill()) {
          extra.push({ text: 'turn off chill mode', next: 'return_chill_off' });
        } else {
          extra.push({ text: 'enable chill mode ♪', next: 'return_chill_on' });
        }
        if (GameProgress?.getSarahUnlockLine()) {
          extra.push({ text: 'how full is the album?', next: 'return_album_pct' });
        }
        return { ...node, choices: [...node.choices, ...extra] };
      }
      if (['orph', 'simon', 'honey'].includes(dialogue.forest) && dialogue.nodeId === 'revisit' && node.lines) {
        const counts = GameProgress?.getFindCounts?.() || JazzStoreOverworld.getFindCounts?.() || {};
        const id = dialogue.forest;
        const n = counts[id] ?? 0;
        const word = JazzStoreOverworld.MUTUAL_FIND_WORDS?.[id] || 'clue';
        if (n > 0 && !node.lines.some((l) => l.includes('find'))) {
          const extra = n >= 3
            ? `you found every ${word} they meant.... they see you noticing.`
            : `find ${word} ${n}/3 — they left hints in the shelves.`;
          return { ...node, lines: [...node.lines, extra] };
        }
      }
      if (dialogue.forest === 'bird' && dialogue.nodeId === 'open' && node.choices) {
        const extra = [];
        if (JazzStoreOverworld.talked?.has?.('orph')) {
          extra.push({ text: 'ask orph — storm-soft air', next: 'bird_orph' });
        }
        if (JazzStoreOverworld.talked?.has?.('simon')) {
          extra.push({ text: 'ask simon — breadcrumb path', next: 'bird_simon' });
        }
        if (JazzStoreOverworld.talked?.has?.('honey')) {
          extra.push({ text: 'ask honey — slow the heartbeat', next: 'bird_honey' });
        }
        if (extra.length) return { ...node, choices: [...node.choices, ...extra] };
      }
      return node;
    }

    function getDialogueNode() {
      const node = DIALOGUE_FORESTS[dialogue.forest]?.[dialogue.nodeId];
      return enrichDialogueNode(node);
    }

    function handleDialogueToken(token) {
      if (token === '__explore__') {
        closeDialogueUI();
        GameProgress?.resetSession?.();
        startOverworld({ freshFinds: true });
        return true;
      }
      if (token === '__start__') {
        clearAftermathReturnTimer();
        clearAftermathDialogueTimer();
        closeDialogueUI();
        JazzStoreOverworld.stop();
        document.getElementById('overworldScene').classList.add('hidden');
        document.body.classList.remove('overworld-active');
        playConfirmBlip();
        startGame();
        return true;
      }
      if (token === '__resume__') {
        closeDialogueUI();
        return true;
      }
      const birdLeave = token.match(/^__bird_leave_(\w+)__$/);
      if (birdLeave) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.resolveBirdEncounter?.(birdLeave[1]);
        closeDialogueUI();
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

    function closeDialogueUI() {
      clearTimeout(dialogue.typeTimer);
      dialogue.typeTimer = null;
      dialogue._afterYou = null;
      dialogue.typing = false;
      dialogue.active = false;
      dScene.classList.add('hidden');
      document.body.classList.remove('dialogue-active');
      VinylEchoBridge?.clearBeatTimer?.();
      if (running || gameStarting || document.body.classList.contains('rhythm-active')) {
        audioBus?.setMode('rhythm');
      } else if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
        audioBus?.setMode('store');
      } else {
        audioBus?.setMode('idle');
      }
    }

    function openDialogueUI(forest, startNode, mode, portrait) {
      clearAftermathDialogueTimer();
      clearTimeout(dialogue.typeTimer);
      dialogue.typeTimer = null;
      dialogue._afterYou = null;
      dialogue.typing = false;
      dialogue.waiting = false;
      VinylEchoBridge?.clearBeatTimer?.();
      initAudio();
      audioBus?.setMode('dialogue');
      dialogue.active = true;
      dialogue.forest = forest;
      dialogue.mode = mode;
      dialogue.portrait = portrait || (
        forest === 'return' || forest === 'aftermath' || forest === 'intro' ? 'ninjawhee' : forest
      );
      dialogue.nodeId = startNode;
      dialogue.lineIdx = 0;
      dialogue.choiceIdx = 0;
      dialogue.emotion = portrait === 'ninjawhee' ? HeavyDialogueArt.emotionForNode(startNode) : 'talk';
      updatePortraitLabel();
      dScene.classList.remove('hidden');
      dScene.classList.toggle('overworld-talk', mode === 'overworld');
      document.body.classList.add('dialogue-active');
      document.getElementById('dialogueHint').textContent = mode === 'overworld'
        ? 'z — advance · return to floor when done'
        : 'z / enter / space — advance · ↑↓ — choose';
      HeavyDialogueArt.whenGrokPortraitReady?.(() => {
        if (dialogue.active && dialogue.portrait === 'ninjawhee') drawDialogueFrame();
      });
      advanceDialogueLine();
      spriteLoop();
    }

    function resolveNpcDialogue(npc, firstTalk) {
      if (npc.tree === 'passerby') {
        const v = npc.variant ?? 0;
        const hintKey = `v${v}_hint`;
        const start = (npc.hasHint && DIALOGUE_FORESTS.passerby?.[hintKey])
          ? hintKey
          : `v${v}`;
        return { forest: 'passerby', start, portrait: 'stranger' };
      }
      if (JazzStoreOverworld.isAftermath?.()) {
        const tier = GameProgress?.getAftermathTier() || 'tasty';
        if (npc.tree === 'return') {
          return { forest: 'aftermath', start: 'aftermath_hub', portrait: 'ninjawhee' };
        }
        const aftermathNode = `aftermath_${tier}`;
        if (DIALOGUE_FORESTS[npc.tree]?.[aftermathNode]) {
          return { forest: npc.tree, start: aftermathNode, portrait: npc.id.replace('_return', '') };
        }
      }
      const forest = npc.tree === 'return' ? 'return' : npc.tree;
      let start;
      if (forest === 'return') {
        const mutualsDone = JazzStoreOverworld.mutualsComplete?.()
          || ['orph', 'simon', 'honey'].every((id) => JazzStoreOverworld.talked?.has?.(id));
        const findsDone = GameProgress?.isFindQuestComplete?.();
        if (!mutualsDone) {
          start = firstTalk ? 'store_open' : 'store_revisit';
        } else if (findsDone) {
          start = 'return_finds_complete';
        } else {
          start = firstTalk ? 'open' : 'return_albums';
        }
      } else {
        start = firstTalk ? 'open' : 'revisit';
        if (!firstTalk) {
          const mutualId = ['orph', 'simon', 'honey'].includes(forest) ? forest : null;
          if (mutualId) {
            const counts = GameProgress?.getFindCounts?.()
              || JazzStoreOverworld.getFindCounts?.() || {};
            const n = Math.min(3, counts[mutualId] || 0);
            if (n >= 1 && DIALOGUE_FORESTS[forest]?.[`revisit_find_${n}`]) {
              start = `revisit_find_${n}`;
            }
          }
          if (start === 'revisit') {
            const vid = JazzStoreOverworld.getLastPreviewVinyl?.();
            if (vid && DIALOGUE_FORESTS[forest]?.[`revisit_${vid}`]) {
              start = `revisit_${vid}`;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      const portrait = forest === 'return' ? 'ninjawhee' : npc.id.replace('_return', '');
      if (!firstTalk && !DIALOGUE_FORESTS[forest]?.[start]) return null;
      return { forest, start, portrait };
    }

    function updateOverworldHintUI(ui) {
      const bar = document.getElementById('overworldHint');
      const keyEl = document.getElementById('owHintKey');
      const actionEl = document.getElementById('owHintAction');
      const targetEl = document.getElementById('owHintTarget');
      const subEl = document.getElementById('owHintSub');
      if (!bar || !ui) return;
      bar.classList.toggle('visible', !!ui.visible && !StorePause?.isOpen?.());
      if (keyEl) {
        if (ui.key) {
          keyEl.textContent = ui.key;
          keyEl.style.display = '';
        } else {
          keyEl.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
      if (actionEl) actionEl.textContent = ui.action || '';
      if (targetEl) targetEl.textContent = ui.target || '';
      if (subEl) subEl.textContent = ui.sub || '';
    }

    function setOverworldPaused(on) {
      StorePause?.[on ? 'open' : 'close']?.();
      JazzStoreOverworld?.setPaused?.(!!on);
      updateOverworldHintUI(JazzStoreOverworld?.buildInteractHintUI?.() || { visible: false });
    }

    function toggleOverworldPause() {
      const next = StorePause?.toggle?.();
      JazzStoreOverworld?.setPaused?.(!!next);
      updateOverworldHintUI(JazzStoreOverworld?.buildInteractHintUI?.() || { visible: false });
      return next;
    }

    async function startOverworld(opts = {}) {
      await ensureAudioReady();
      audioBus?.setMode('store');
      audioBus?.ensureVinylAudible?.(AudioBus.VINYL_STORE_LEVEL);
      document.body.classList.remove('rhythm-active');
      document.getElementById('overworldScene').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.body.classList.add('overworld-active');
      StorePause?.init?.();
      StorePause?.bindDom?.();
      StorePause?.close?.();
      clearAftermathDialogueTimer();
      EasterEggs?.resetSession?.();
      StoreEvents?.resetSession?.();
      cleanupRhythmSession();
      if (opts.aftermath) VinylEchoBridge?.cleanup?.();
      else VinylEchoBridge?.resetSession?.();
      if (JazzStoreOverworld.isAftermath?.() && opts.aftermath) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.stop();
      }
      JazzStoreOverworld.start(document.getElementById('overworldCanvas'), {
        aftermath: opts.aftermath || null,
        onInteractHint: updateOverworldHintUI,
        onReturnReady: () => {
          initAudio();
          audioBus?.resume();
          HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, masterGain, 'choice');
          const echoes = VinylEchoBridge?.getVinylListenCount?.() || 0;
          if (echoes < 1) {
            JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.('spin a vinyl first — echoes make richer bites ♫', 3600);
          }
        },
        onAftermathEnter: (tier) => {
          initAudio();
          audioBus?.resume();
          const motifId = VinylEchoBridge?.getLastVinylId?.()
            || GameProgress?.getState?.()?.vinyls?.slice(-1)[0] || 'moon';
          VinylEchoBridge?.playMotif?.(motifId, audioCtx, masterGain, 0.055);
          JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.('...reminds me of that one', 3400);
        },
        onSecretInteract: onOverworldSecret,
        onTalkNPC: (npc, firstTalk = true) => {
          const route = resolveNpcDialogue(npc, firstTalk);
          if (!route) return;
          if (!npc.isPasserby && !JazzStoreOverworld.isAftermath?.()) {
            VinylEchoBridge?.recordNpc(npc.id.replace('_return', ''));
            GameProgress?.recordNpc(npc.id.replace('_return', ''));
          }
          StorePause?.onTalk?.(npc, firstTalk);
          if (npc.isPasserby) StoreItems?.tryPickupFromTalk?.(npc);
          openDialogueUI(route.forest, route.start, 'overworld', route.portrait);
        },
        onTalkBird: () => openDialogueUI('bird', 'open', 'overworld', 'bird'),
        onBirdGuide: () => {
          initAudio();
          if (audioCtx?.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
          VinylEchoBridge?.recordBirdGuide?.(audioCtx, masterGain);
        },
        onListenVinyl: async (vinyl) => {
          await ensureAudioReady();
          VinylAudio?.init(audioCtx, audioBus.vinylGain);
          audioBus?.setMode('store');
          await audioBus?.resumeAndResetGain?.(AudioBus.VINYL_STORE_LEVEL);
          if (audioCtx?.state === 'suspended') await audioCtx.resume();
          HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, masterGain, 'vinyl');
          const vinylNew = !GameProgress?.hasVinyl?.(vinyl.id);
          const title = await VinylAudio?.play(vinyl.id);
          if (!title) {
            JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.('audio asleep — tap [Z] again', 2400);
            return null;
          }
          VinylEchoBridge?.recordPreview(vinyl.id, audioCtx, masterGain);
          GameProgress?.recordVinyl(vinyl.id);
          StorePause?.onVinyl?.(vinyl.id, vinylNew);
          if (vinylNew) VinylEchoBridge?.showGhostSlice?.(vinyl.id, audioCtx, masterGain);
          const listens = GameProgress?.getVinylListenCount?.(vinyl.id) ?? 0;
          if (VinylEchoBridge?.trySarahHum?.(vinyl.id, audioCtx, masterGain)) {
            JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.('sarah hums along.... she used to love this one', 3600);
          } else if (listens === 3) {
            JazzStoreOverworld.showSecretToast?.('three spins on this one.... sarah used to hum it with me', 3200);
          }
          handleEggUnlock(EasterEggs?.onVinylPreview?.(vinyl.id), { blip: false });
          return title;
        },
      });
      StorePause?.onSessionStart?.({ aftermath: opts.aftermath || null });
      updateOverworldHintUI(JazzStoreOverworld?.buildInteractHintUI?.() || { visible: true });
      if (opts.openSarahDialogue && opts.aftermath) {
        clearAftermathDialogueTimer();
        handleEggUnlock(EasterEggs?.onAftermathEnter?.(opts.aftermath.tier), { blip: false });
        aftermathDialogueTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          aftermathDialogueTimer = null;
          if (!document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) return;
          if (dialogue.active) return;
          openDialogueUI('aftermath', opts.aftermath.tier, 'overworld', 'ninjawhee');
        }, 520);
      }
    }

    async function enterAftermathStore() {
      if (enteringAftermath || aftermathTransitioning) return;
      clearAftermathReturnTimer();
      resetMirrorFlow();
      if (dialogue.active) closeDialogueUI();
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active') && JazzStoreOverworld.isAftermath?.()) {
        document.getElementById('endOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
        return;
      }
      enteringAftermath = true;
      aftermathTransitioning = true;
      try {
        document.getElementById('endOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('introOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
        rhythmLoop?.stopClean?.() ?? rhythmLoop?.stop?.();
        cleanupRhythmSession();
        stopQuoteCycler();
        clearTimeout(phaseHideTimer);
        running = false;
        gameStarting = false;
        document.body.classList.remove('rhythm-active');
        audioBus?.setMode('store');
        JazzStoreOverworld.stop();

        const lastRun = GameProgress?.getLastRun();
        const tier = GameProgress?.getAftermathTier(lastRun) || 'tasty';
        await startOverworld({
          aftermath: { tier, ...(lastRun || {}) },
          openSarahDialogue: true,
        });
      } finally {
        enteringAftermath = false;
        aftermathTransitioning = false;
      }
    }

    function showSpeaker(name) {
      dSpeaker.textContent = name;
      dSpeaker.classList.toggle('you', name === 'you');
    }

    function hideChoices() {
      dChoices.classList.remove('active');
      dChoices.innerHTML = '';
      dLine.style.display = 'block';
    }

    function renderChoices(choices) {
      dialogue.waiting = true;
      dialogue.typing = false;
      dCursor.classList.remove('show');
      dLine.style.display = 'none';
      dChoices.classList.add('active');
      dChoices.innerHTML = choices.map((c, i) =>
        `<div class="choice-item${i === dialogue.choiceIdx ? ' selected' : ''}" data-i="${i}">
          <span class="heart">♥</span>${c.text}
        </div>`
      ).join('');
      dChoices.querySelectorAll('.choice-item').forEach((el) => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          dialogue.choiceIdx = Number(el.dataset.i);
          confirmChoice();
        });
      });
    }

    function resolveDialogueLine(text) {
      if (text === '__album_pct__' && GameProgress) {
        const pct = GameProgress.getAlbumPct();
        const explore = GameProgress.getExplorationPct();
        const unlock = GameProgress.getSarahUnlockLine();
        return `album ${pct}% toward whole. store explored ${explore}%.${unlock ? ` ${unlock}` : ''}`;
      }
      if (text === '__secret_count__' && GameProgress) {
        const n = GameProgress.getSecretCount();
        return `${n} secret${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} tucked in the stacks. the album sees all of them.`;
      }
      if (text === '__run_stats__' && GameProgress) {
        const r = GameProgress.getLastRun();
        const tier = GameProgress.getAftermathTier(r);
        if (!r) return 'no needle drop remembered yet.';
        const label = { wings: 'WINGS', groove: 'GROOVE', tasty: 'TASTY', static: 'STATIC' }[tier] || tier;
        const progress = r.won ? 'fifteen slices · mirror touched' : `${r.slices}/15 slices · needle up early`;
        return `${label} run · ${progress} · score ${r.score} · ${r.perfects} SLICE! · improv ${r.improv || 0}%`;
      }
      if (text === '__groove_choice_line__' && GameProgress) {
        const r = GameProgress.getLastRun();
        if (!r?.won) return '';
        if (r.grooveChoice === 'pass') {
          return 'you passed the groove on.... the store will hum it for the next walker....';
        }
        return 'you kept the groove.... i can still hear it in the glass....';
      }
      return text;
    }

    function beginLine(text) {
      dialogue.fullLine = resolveDialogueLine(text);
      dialogue.charIdx = 0;
      dialogue.typing = true;
      dialogue.waiting = false;
      dialogue.typeDeadline = Date.now() + 800;
      dCursor.classList.remove('show');
      dLine.textContent = '';
      hideChoices();
      dLine.style.display = 'block';
      clearTimeout(dialogue.typeTimer);
      dialogue.typeTimer = setTimeout(dialogueTick, 30);
    }

    function advanceDialogueLine() {
      const node = getDialogueNode();
      if (!node) return closeDialogueUI();

      if (dialogue.forest === 'bird' && ['orph', 'simon', 'honey'].includes(node.speaker)) {
        dialogue.portrait = node.speaker;
        updatePortraitLabel();
      }
      if (dialogue.portrait === 'ninjawhee') {
        dialogue.emotion = HeavyDialogueArt.emotionForNode(dialogue.nodeId);
      }
      if (node.lines && dialogue.lineIdx < node.lines.length) {
        showSpeaker(node.speaker || dialogue.portrait);
        beginLine(node.lines[dialogue.lineIdx]);
        dialogue.lineIdx++;
        return;
      }

      if (node.choices) {
        renderChoices(node.choices);
        return;
      }

      if (node.next && handleDialogueToken(node.next)) return;

      if (node.next) {
        dialogue.nodeId = node.next;
        dialogue.lineIdx = 0;
        advanceDialogueLine();
      } else {
        closeDialogueUI();
      }
    }

    function confirmChoice() {
      const node = getDialogueNode();
      if (!node?.choices) return;
      const pick = node.choices[dialogue.choiceIdx];
      if (!pick) return;
      playConfirmBlip();
      hideChoices();
      if (pick.next === 'return_chill_on') GameProgress?.setChill(true);
      if (pick.next === 'return_chill_off') GameProgress?.setChill(false);
      if (handleDialogueToken(pick.next)) return;
      showSpeaker('you');
      beginLine(`* ${pick.text}`);
      dialogue._afterYou = () => {
        dialogue._afterYou = null;
        dialogue.nodeId = pick.next;
        dialogue.lineIdx = 0;
        dialogue.choiceIdx = 0;
        showSpeaker(dialogue.portrait);
        updatePortraitLabel();
        advanceDialogueLine();
      };
    }

    function dialogueAdvance() {
      if (!dialogue.active) return;
      initAudio();
      if (audioCtx?.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();

      if (dChoices.classList.contains('active')) {
        confirmChoice();
        return;
      }

      if (dialogue.typing) {
        dialogue.typing = false;
        clearTimeout(dialogue.typeTimer);
        dLine.textContent = dialogue.fullLine;
        dialogue.waiting = true;
        dCursor.classList.add('show');
        HeavyDialogueArt.playUndertaleBlip(audioCtx, masterGain, 'advance');
        return;
      }

      if (dialogue.waiting) {
        dialogue.waiting = false;
        dCursor.classList.remove('show');
        if (dialogue._afterYou) {
          dialogue._afterYou();
          return;
        }
        advanceDialogueLine();
      }
    }

    function dialogueTick() {
      if (!dialogue.active || !dialogue.typing) return;
      if (dialogue.charIdx >= dialogue.fullLine.length) {
        dialogue.typing = false;
        dialogue.waiting = true;
        dCursor.classList.add('show');
        return;
      }
      const ch = dialogue.fullLine[dialogue.charIdx++];
      dLine.textContent += ch;
      if (ch.trim()) playDialogueBlip();
      if (dialogue.typeDeadline && Date.now() >= dialogue.typeDeadline) {
        dLine.textContent = dialogue.fullLine;
        dialogue.charIdx = dialogue.fullLine.length;
        dialogue.typing = false;
        dialogue.waiting = true;
        dCursor.classList.add('show');
        return;
      }
      const delay = ch === '.' || ch === ',' ? 55 : ch === ' ' ? 14 : 20;
      dialogue.typeTimer = setTimeout(dialogueTick, delay);
    }

    function openBootIntro() {
      const gp = GameProgress?.getState?.();
      const explore = GameProgress?.getExplorationPct?.() ?? 0;
      if (gp?.runs > 0 || gp?.wins > 0) {
        VinylEchoBridge?.resetSession?.();
        openDialogueUI('intro', 'returning_visitor', 'cinematic', 'ninjawhee');
        return;
      }
      openDialogueUI('intro', 'open', 'cinematic', 'ninjawhee');
    }

    function bootGame() {
      document.getElementById('introOverlay').classList.add('hidden');
      const begin = () => {
        HeavyDialogueArt.whenGrokPortraitReady?.(() => {
          if (dialogue.active && dialogue.portrait === 'ninjawhee') drawDialogueFrame();
        });
        openBootIntro();
      };
      if (typeof HeavyDialogueArt.whenGrokPortraitReady === 'function') {
        if (HeavyDialogueArt.isGrokPortraitReady?.()) begin();
        else HeavyDialogueArt.whenGrokPortraitReady(begin);
      } else {
        begin();
      }
    }

    function startDialogue(skip = false) {
      dialogue.skipIntro = skip;
      if (skip) {
        closeDialogueUI();
        return;
      }
      bootGame();
    }

    function moveChoice(dir) {
      if (!dChoices.classList.contains('active')) return;
      const node = getDialogueNode();
      if (!node?.choices) return;
      dialogue.choiceIdx = (dialogue.choiceIdx + dir + node.choices.length) % node.choices.length;
      playChoiceBlip();
      dChoices.querySelectorAll('.choice-item').forEach((el, i) => {
        el.classList.toggle('selected', i === dialogue.choiceIdx);
      });
    }

    document.getElementById('dialogueBox').addEventListener('click', dialogueAdvance);

    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (dialogue.active) {
        if (['KeyZ', 'Enter', 'Space'].includes(e.code)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          dialogueAdvance();
          return;
        }
        if (e.code === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); moveChoice(-1); }
        if (e.code === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); moveChoice(1); }
        if (e.code === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (dialogue.mode === 'overworld') closeDialogueUI();
          else if (dialogue.forest === 'intro') handleDialogueToken('__explore__');
          else closeDialogueUI();
        }
        return;
      }
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
        if (e.code === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          toggleOverworldPause();
          return;
        }
        if (StorePause?.isOpen?.()) return;
        if (JazzStoreOverworld.handleKey(e.code, { repeat: e.repeat })) e.preventDefault();
      }
    }, true);

    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      if (dialogue.active) return;
      if (document.body.classList.contains('overworld-active')) {
        JazzStoreOverworld.handleKeyUp(e.code);
      }
    }, true);

    const _startGame = startGame;
    startGame = async function (...args) {
      clearAftermathReturnTimer();
      clearAftermathDialogueTimer();
      closeDialogueUI();
      StorePause?.close?.();
      JazzStoreOverworld.stop();
      document.getElementById('overworldScene').classList.add('hidden');
      document.body.classList.remove('overworld-active');
      document.getElementById('overworldHint')?.classList.remove('visible');
      return _startGame.apply(this, args);
    };

    StorePause?.init?.();
    StorePause?.bindDom?.();

    const chillToggle = document.getElementById('chillToggle');
    if (chillToggle && GameProgress) {
      chillToggle.checked = GameProgress.isChill();
      chillToggle.addEventListener('change', () => {
        GameProgress.setChill(chillToggle.checked);
        updateAlbumPctIntro();
      });
    }
    updateAlbumPctIntro();

    bootGame();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

// Hear Records–inspired pixel overworld (Burlington Square, Singapore)
window.JazzStoreOverworld = (function () {
  const TILE = 28;
  const ROOM_W = 22;
  const COLS = ROOM_W * 3;
  const ROWS = 13;

  function patchRow(row, col, ch = '.') {
    return row.slice(0, col) + ch + row.slice(col + 1);
  }

  // Interior only — full perimeter W (no door gap · no walk-through bottom)
  function buildOpenStoreMap() {
    const map = [];
    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {
      let row = '';
      for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {
        const perimeter = y === 0 || y === ROWS - 1 || x === 0 || x === COLS - 1;
        row += perimeter ? 'W' : '.';
      }
      map.push(row);
    }
    return map;
  }

  const MAP = buildOpenStoreMap();

  const ARCH_ROWS = { min: 1, max: 11 };
  const ARCH_COLS = [ROOM_W - 1, ROOM_W, ROOM_W * 2 - 1, ROOM_W * 2];

  function validateMap() {
    const issues = [];
    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {
      if (MAP[y].length !== COLS) {
        throw new Error(`MAP row ${y} length ${MAP[y].length} expected ${COLS}`);
      }
    }
    for (let gy = 0; gy < ROWS; gy++) {
      for (let gx = 0; gx < COLS; gx++) {
        const t = MAP[gy][gx];
        if (!'WSCD.RTP'.includes(t)) issues.push(`bad char "${t}" at ${gx},${gy}`);
      }
    }
    for (let gy = 1; gy < ROWS - 1; gy++) {
      for (let gx = 1; gx < COLS - 1; gx++) {
        const t = MAP[gy][gx];
        if (t === 'W' || t === 'S' || t === 'C') {
          issues.push(`interior blocker at ${gx},${gy} = ${t}`);
        }
      }
    }
    for (const v of VINYL_PICKUPS) {
      if (!isWalkableTile(MAP[v.padY][v.padX])) {
        issues.push(`vinyl pad blocked ${v.id}@${v.padX},${v.padY}`);
      }
    }
    for (const spot of EXAMINE_SPOTS) {
      if (spot.padX != null && !isWalkableTile(MAP[spot.padY][spot.padX])) {
        issues.push(`examine pad blocked ${spot.id}@${spot.padX},${spot.padY}`);
      }
    }
    if (issues.length) {
      console.warn('[overworld] validateMap:', issues.join('; '));
    }
  }

  const PLAYER_WALK_GRID = new Uint8Array(COLS * ROWS);
  const NPC_WALK_GRID = new Uint8Array(COLS * ROWS);

  function rebuildWalkGrids() {
    for (let gy = 0; gy < ROWS; gy++) {
      for (let gx = 0; gx < COLS; gx++) {
        const t = MAP[gy][gx];
        const idx = gy * COLS + gx;
        // Player: only visible perimeter walls block — props/NPCs never block
        PLAYER_WALK_GRID[idx] = t !== 'W' ? 1 : 0;
        NPC_WALK_GRID[idx] = (t !== 'W' && t !== 'S' && t !== 'C') || t === 'D' ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }
  }

  const BIRD_PERCH = { x: 8, y: 2 };
